PS C:\Windows\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0> $base = "$env:APPDATA\Antigravity"
>>
>> Write-Host "`n============================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
>> Write-Host " LOGS DO ANTIGRAVITY - ERROS 429 / QUOTA" -ForegroundColor Cyan
>> Write-Host "============================================`n" -ForegroundColor Cyan
>>
>> $patterns = @(
>>     "429",
>>     "RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED",
>>     "Too Many Requests",
>>     "quota",
>>     "rate limit",
>>     "resource exhausted",
>>     "Trajectory",
>>     "TraceID",
>>     "Cloudaicompanion",
>>     "cloudcode",
>>     "streamGenerateContent"
>> )
>>
>> $files = Get-ChildItem "$base\logs" -Recurse -File -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
>>
>> Write-Host "Arquivos de log encontrados: $($files.Count)`n" -ForegroundColor Yellow
>>
>> foreach ($pattern in $patterns) {
>>
>>     Write-Host "===== PROCURANDO: $pattern =====" -ForegroundColor Green
>>
>>     $matches = $files | Select-String `
>>         -Pattern $pattern `
>>         -SimpleMatch `
>>         -CaseSensitive:$false `
>>         -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
>>
>>     if ($matches) {
>>         $matches |
>>             Select-Object Path, LineNumber, Line |
>>             Format-List
>>     }
>>     else {
>>         Write-Host "Nenhuma ocorrência encontrada." -ForegroundColor DarkGray
>>     }
>>
>>     Write-Host ""
>> }
>>
>> Write-Host "============================================" -ForegroundColor Cyan
>> Write-Host " ARQUIVOS DE LOG MAIS RECENTES" -ForegroundColor Cyan
>> Write-Host "============================================`n" -ForegroundColor Cyan
>>
>> $files |
>>     Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending |
>>     Select-Object -First 30 LastWriteTime, Length, FullName |
>>     Format-Table -AutoSize
